Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a interpolation algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 345. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 103/2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to interpolation structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 345/103 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the interpolation structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 963. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 531 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 963 + O(1/n^531). Summing over all modes and applying the interpolation structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 215.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For any interpolation module M satisfying the invariance principle hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 132. The projective resolution has length at most 370 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.
